1-\frac{I_2(x)}{I_1(x)}>\frac{1}{x+1}.
$$

On a finite connected graph or multigraph with independent \(SU(2)\) link coordinates, product Haar measure, the full vertex gauge group, and at least one cycle, every nonconstant spin-network support contains a cycle. In the absence of matter, fixed links, flatness or holonomy constraints, and charged boundaries, a fundamental Wilson character on a shortest cycle saturates the resulting estimate, so

$$
\left\|P_{x,\Gamma}(I-P_0)\right\|
=
\left(\frac{I_2(x)}{I_1(x)}\right)^{g(\Gamma)},
\qquad
\Delta_{K,\Gamma}
=
\frac{\hbar c}{a_\tau}g(\Gamma)
\log\frac{I_1(x)}{I_2(x)}.
$$

Here the lattice Laplacian is normalized by \(D\chi_j=j(j+1)\chi_j\), so its fundamental Casimir is \(3/4\); it is twice the \(-B_{\mathfrak{su}(2)}\)-metric Laplacian used by default in the general girth theorem. In the \(3+1\)-dimensional tree-level anisotropic convention, with unnormalized fundamental trace and \(x=4a_s/(g_0^2a_\tau)\), the fixed-\(a_s,g_0\) temporal limit is

$$
\Delta_{K,\Gamma}
\longrightarrow
\frac{g_0^2\hbar c}{2a_s}\frac{3g(\Gamma)}4,
$$

which is exactly the Kogut--Susskind electric coefficient times the gauge-invariant Haar girth--Casimir floor. This is a pure-kinetic normalization theorem, not an interacting glueball mass or a continuum gap.

For the full finite Wilson sandwich \(T=M_aKM_a\), let \(T\psi=\lambda_0\psi\), \(\rho=\psi^2\), \(0<a_-\leq a\leq a_+\), and \(0<\rho_-\leq\rho\leq\rho_+\). The ground-state edge identity, \(\lambda_0\leq a_+^2\), and variance comparison give the exact finite-volume estimate

$$
\operatorname{gap}(P_T)
\geq
\operatorname{gap}(K|_{\mathrm{GI}})
\left(\frac{a_-}{a_+}\right)^2
\frac{\rho_-}{\rho_+}.
$$

If the product kernel satisfies \(0<k_-\leq k\leq k_+\), the Perron equation further gives the action-only bound with factor \((a_-/a_+)^4(k_-/k_+)^2\). For the \(SU(2)\) Wilson product, \(k_-/k_+=e^{-2x|E_\Gamma|}\). On a regular fixed-\(x\) lattice family this certificate therefore deteriorates exponentially with spatial volume. This is useful negative information: a local or block-conditional estimate is a viable replacement for the failed global minimum-to-maximum density certificate, although the calculation does not exclude every possible global comparison.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/finite-spacing-transfer-and-bounded-flux-solder|The general fixed-spacing solder target]] is therefore bounded. [[markov-edge-measure-solder/inq|Stationary two-slice measure domination]] is a sufficient noncircular certificate and survives finite tensor products without multiplying one loss factor per component. If an independently derived cylinder generator satisfies

$$
D_{\mathrm{cyl},r}
\geq
\lambda_{\mathrm{cyl},r}(I-P_{0,r})
$$

and a direct action-, kernel-, or RG-level comparison proves

$$
I-P_{T,r}
\geq
\eta_r\left(I-e^{-\tau_rD_{\mathrm{cyl},r}}\right),
\qquad
\eta_r>0,
$$

with \(\tau_rD_{\mathrm{cyl},r}\) dimensionless, put

$$
q_r
:=
\eta_r\left(1-e^{-\tau_r\lambda_{\mathrm{cyl},r}}\right).
$$

Strict positivity of the transfer forces \(q_r<1\) on a nonzero vacuum complement, and spectral calculus gives the sharper rate

$$
\Delta_{T,r}
\geq
\frac{\hbar c}{a_{\tau,r}}\,
\log\left((1-q_r)^{-1}\right)
\geq
\frac{\hbar c}{a_{\tau,r}}q_r.
$$

If \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{YM}}^{(\mathsf s)}\) is the renormalized energy scale in scheme \(\mathsf s\), the regulator-independent stopping quantity is

$$
\boxed{
\liminf_{\substack{a_{\tau,r}\downarrow0\\L_r\uparrow\infty}}
\frac{\hbar c}{a_{\tau,r}\Lambda_{\mathrm{YM}}^{(\mathsf s)}}\,
\log\left((1-q_r)^{-1}\right)
>0.}
$$

This—not an \(a_\tau\)-independent raw lattice edge—is the required finite-transfer estimate. A finite continuum rate normally has \(q_r=O(a_{\tau,r})\), in which regime the logarithmic and linear bounds are asymptotically equivalent; the logarithm is the exact stopping quantity. Defining \(\eta_r\) from the unknown excited spectrum would be circular. A second branch begins with a genuine continuous-time Hamiltonian whose physical form is already known on a common form core; there an unbounded comparison \(h_r\geq\chi_r\mathcal E_r\) remains valid if proved. It cannot be imported backward to a fixed-spacing Wilson transfer matrix. Both branches require comparison maps between varying carriers, convergence of vacuum projections and forms, and identification of the limit with a nontrivial local theory. Only after Poincare reconstruction may the result be rewritten as a mass Casimir floor.

A distinct exact upstream member is now realized in [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/pointing-coercivity-and-the-flat-partner-law|pointing coercivity and the flat-partner law]]. On the commutative core-scale shadow, and under the theorem's real whole-line regularity and ordered-kernel hypotheses, the normalized dual-translation derivation and homogeneous-partner assumption turn one normalizable pointing into a logistic state with sharp dimensionless edge \(\nu^2\). Extending [[wall-construction-interface/scale-character-solder|the scale-character solder]] reveals two strict identities, not one: capacity matched to projection-coded log odds gives \(\nu=1/2\), while capacity matched to the normalized-involution parameter gives \(\nu=1\). Within the logistic family, the separate nondegenerate incoming core-density axiom marks the critical value and hence the limiting candidate edge \(1/4\); the relative-zero-mode admissibility rule instead selects the strict post-crossing phase \(\nu>1/2\). These are not cumulative static constraints: a scale-evolution or carrier-formation map must connect them. Neither rule selects a physical readout generator. This still is not mass.

At finite regulator the physical analysis map is no longer wholly abstract. Under the stated reflection-Markov, reflection-fixed-separator, and dense-interface-insertion hypotheses, the OS quotient factors through a unitary \(B_\rho^{\mathrm{OS}}:\mathcal H_{\mathrm{OS}}\to L^2(\nu_{\rho,I})^{\mathrm{GI}}\) with exact reference-complement coverage. Calling that line the complete vacuum requires infinite-depth preparation and uniqueness; calling the differentiated map complete further requires that the closed interface gradient have kernel exactly \(\mathbb C1\). A thick interface still requires an interface-to-transfer-slice map before its gradient may be called electric flux. At the vacuum-prepared stage the logistic route then has the sharper factorization

$$
\mathcal J_{\mathrm{sc},\rho}
=
S_\rho B_\rho^{\mathrm{OS}}(1-P_{0,\rho}),
$$

The coverage portion of \(S_\rho\) is now no longer wholly open. If a nonnegative self-adjoint interface operator \(L_{\rho,I}\) is constructed independently with \(\ker L_{\rho,I}=\mathbb C1\), choose an independently normalized positive reference value \(L_{*,\rho}\) and put \(\widehat L_{\rho,I}:=L_{\rho,I}/L_{*,\rho}\). [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/resolvent-logistic-scale-transform|The resolvent-logistic theorem]] then gives the exact complex-linear isometry

$$
(S_\rho^{\mathrm{res}}f)(N)
=
\frac{(e^N\widehat L_{\rho,I})^{1/2}}
{1+e^N\widehat L_{\rho,I}}f
$$

on the reference complement. It is unitary-natural and converts multiplication of \(\widehat L\) into translation in \(N\). Each spectral channel is a translated logistic half-density with \(\nu=1/2\) when \(N\) logs the multiplicative parameter. The theorem simultaneously blocks a false completion: the transform has the same profile and exact coverage for a gapless operator, with small spectral values merely moving their centers to \(+\infty\). Its natural log-Haar half-density codomain is not the fixed-center weighted probability carrier above, and the universal \(1/4\) shape edge is not mass. A physical gap is instead equivalent to a uniform ceiling on \(-\log\widehat L\), or to norm-uniform rather than merely strong effect saturation. Selection of \(L_{\rho,I}\), preservation of regional localization, the codomain comparison, the center ceiling, and the independent full-Casimir solder remain open. An abstract unitary or an \(L\) chosen after inspecting the physical spectrum is inadmissible.

The RG-shell suggestion can now be sharpened without inventing weights. If a state-preserving regulator filtration has GNS shell projections \(D_{\rho,j}\) and independently calibrated addresses \(N_{\rho,j}\), [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/paired-scale-filtrations-and-the-invariant-incidence-wall|the paired-filtration theorem]] constructs \(A_{\rho,\mathcal F}=\sum_jN_{\rho,j}D_{\rho,j}\) and \(L_{\rho,\mathcal F}=0\oplus e^{-A_{\rho,\mathcal F}}\), after which the resolvent transform supplies the continuous scale analysis. This construction also proves that one tower whose addresses are unbounded above is necessarily gapless. For two cross-commuting towers, the product floor is instead equivalent to a diagonal ceiling on the support of their joint shell projections, so unbounded relative resolution can coexist with bounded invariant mean address. The terminal tail of each tower must equal the vacuum; equality only of their intersection leaves nonvacuum one-sided zero modes. A single physical null-pair product \(Q_{\mathbf n}=P_+(\mathbf n)P_-(\mathbf n)\) is not pointwise equal to the four-dimensional mass Casimir because it includes transverse momentum. Nevertheless, on the full vacuum complement of a positive-energy Poincare representation, \(Q_{\mathbf n}\geq\Delta^2(I-P_0)\), \(H\geq\Delta(I-P_0)\), and \(\mathcal C\geq\Delta^2(I-P_0)\) are equivalent for any one fixed \(\mathbf n\). A common-floor family on any countable dense set of directions gives the exact direct pointwise tomography of \(\mathcal C\), but is not necessary merely to infer the gap. Filtration products still require a separate full-carrier physical null-translation solder. None of these exact implications supplies the incidence ceiling, its dimensional normalization, or its Yang--Mills continuum stability.

The hard support ceiling now has an exact soft-wall alternative. [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/compensated-incidence-response-and-four-dimensional-balance|The compensated-response theorem]] composes the invariant scale presentation \(M_p=e^{-pA}\) with an independently derived positive response \(R\). In a shell-reducing realization the squared floor is \(\inf_\alpha\rho_\alpha e^{-2pa_\alpha}\); without commutation it is the closed-range inequality for \(\overline{R^{1/2}M_p}\). After quotienting its vacuum kernel, the floor is equivalently bounded invertibility onto a closed image, so no normalized nonvacuum distinction can approach null response. Hence a deep joint incidence may remain realizable while its presented image is charged strongly enough to prevent arbitrarily cheap distinction. On a bilateral scale carrier the homogeneous law \(R=e^{qA}\) succeeds only at \(q=2p\). If a codimension-two response independently has homogeneous power character \(q=D-2\), first inverse-length presentation is power-neutral at \(D=4\); unique dimensional selection additionally requires bilateral support and two-sided control of the residual response. In that dimension the scale-diagonal quantum ansatz \(R=e^{2A}Z_4(A)\) leaves the exact obligation \(\operatorname*{ess\,inf}_{E_A}Z_4>0\): power cancellation does not prove infrared coercivity. This may re-express ordinary four-dimensional Yang--Mills power counting; it does not derive the address/log-length solder, response, dimensional transmutation scale, continuum theory, or Casimir comparison.

The causal-grain fossil proposal now has one exact reduced memory mechanism and one unproved historical identification. The translation-Haar relative-density operator crosses a non-Fredholm boundary and its post-crossing Fredholm index is stable under continuous deformations that remain Fredholm. That integer—not a femtometre cell—is what can literally persist in the reduced mathematics. If physical admissibility is additionally declared to require its normalizable zero mode, the allowed scale-shadow members occur only for \(\nu>1/2\) and are born with probability stiffness \(>1/4\), with onset infimum \(1/4\); the carrier rule, not the algebra, is the unproved selection. The critical incoming-density law and this strict admissibility rule describe the transition locus and its post-crossing phase, respectively. On a declared \(C^1\) pre-clock scale family, a transverse crossing of \(\varepsilon(s)=2\nu_s-\chi_s\) makes the sequence index \(0\), non-Fredholm threshold, index \(1\) exact, and \(\inf_{s>s_c}\chi_s>0\) gives a uniform positive probability-stiffness floor; deriving that history and carrier rule remains open. Identifying the class with the historically prior primitive wall index, transporting it through \(S_\rho\), and pairing it with a CMB or BAO observable are separate open constructions. The displayed fm/MeV values use a late-time absolute normalization and are downstream presentations, not an early timestamp.

The standard cosmological equations add a second, independent memory mechanism. With \(N=\log a\) and \(\Theta=\rho-3p\), total stress conservation gives

$$
\frac{\mathrm d}{\mathrm dN}(e^{4N}\rho)=e^{4N}\Theta.
$$

The trace is therefore exactly the source for failure of radiation's \(a^{-4}\) scaling. Dividing by \(\rho>0\) yields the dimensionless cocycle \(\Xi_\Theta(N_1,N_2)=\int_{N_1}^{N_2}(\Theta/\rho)\,\mathrm dN=\Delta\log(a^4\rho)\), which is additive under interval concatenation and invariant under translation of the arbitrary origin of \(N\). It shares the additive composition law of an abstract scale valuation, but acts on a different domain; it is an endpoint coboundary, need not be quantized, and has no proven wall origin. At fixed initial radiation-normalized density, its first variation has kernel \(K_{\Xi\leftarrow\Theta}(s)=e^{4(s-N_d)}/\rho(N_d)\). Composing the unnormalized density perturbation with \(\delta H_{\mathrm{FLRW}}/H_{\mathrm{FLRW}}=\delta\rho/(2\rho)\) in spatially flat Einstein evolution and holding loading and drag fixed produces a different, negative kernel from \(\delta\Theta\) to \(\delta r_d\). A nonconformal source profile can therefore leave distinguishable integrated images after the source is gone, but neither image alone identifies it. Full visibility still depends on the correlated \(R_b\), opacity, drag, active-stress, abundance, and primordial-state responses. The Yang--Mills trace anomaly identifies a structurally relevant dilation obstruction, not a proof that its vacuum spectrum is gapped or that it generated the acoustic fossil.

The fossil vocabulary now has four typed levels. The primitive engagement class is the proposed pre-clock transition datum; the metric/common-count branch sends it toward \((\lambda_{\mathrm{cc}},Q_c,\Sigma_c)\); the material branch first supplies an intermediate trace and constitutive history; ordinary transfer then returns CMB/BAO observables. Their common origin is open. A reconvergence theorem must derive both branches from one upstream carrier and compare their normalizations without reading either the observed gap or acoustic ruler backward. More generally, a finite family of frozen response functionals on an unrestricted infinite-dimensional history carrier determines only the quotient by an infinite-dimensional kernel. On an independently derived finite-dimensional profile space \(V\), it becomes injective exactly when \(V\cap\ker\mathfrak F=\{0\}\). The standard sound-horizon map also has the explicit cancellation direction \(\delta H_{\mathrm{FLRW}}/H_{\mathrm{FLRW}}=-\delta R_b/[2(1+R_b)]\) at fixed drag endpoint. A shell-casing claim therefore requires more than separation of pre- and post-engagement observable sets: the tuples consisting of metric calibration, intermediate history signatures, and transported observable images must form positively separated joint-descendant sets after common nuisance histories are marginalized. A predeclared shared-signature intertwiner is a further condition; it establishes causal common origin only when both descendant maps are independently constructed from one carrier and the signature is complete enough for the claimed identification. Locally, the engagement derivative must survive projection away from the nuisance tangent. BAO alone cannot identify the transition.

The pair-annihilation quotient now supplies one exact algebraic carrier template for “what could the physical carrier be?” On the comoving population cone, pair reactions occupy the diagonal kernel, the signed baryon difference is the quotient residue, and the complete-annihilation retraction to one oriented axis is deterministic but noninvertible; it is a least-population selector, not the generic terminal result of finite-rate kinetics. The symmetric load is forgotten only by the reduced baryon carrier, so no claim of microscopic information destruction follows. Every pair-only reaction generator kills observables of the retained difference, and its relaxation edge is therefore vertical rather than the residual sector's mass. With conserved comoving entropy, \(Y_B=(n_B-n_{\bar B})/s\) is the dimensionless retained charge. The material map first folds \(Y_B\mapsto|Y_B|\), then supplies mass, composition, dilution, and positive inertial loading. Standard acoustic transfer can depend on that loading but cannot recover the orientation or annihilation trajectory. The typed chain is

$$
\text{cancelable direction}
\longrightarrow
\text{quotient residue}
\longrightarrow
\text{charge-even material loading}
\longrightarrow
\text{many-to-one acoustic readout}.
$$

It is a model for the desired Yang--Mills architecture, not its solution. The missing Yang--Mills theorem must replace the pair kernel by the correct gauge/descent kernel, construct a positive form on the entire retained vacuum complement, and solder its uniform edge to the reconstructed Poincare Casimir.

[[wall-crossing-defect-and-the-fossil-of-mass-engagement|The wall-crossing defect]] sharpens the word “event.” If \(J_\sigma\) maps a pre-wall carrier into a post-wall carrier with generators \(A_-\) and \(A_+\), then

$$
\mathfrak D_\sigma
=
J_\sigma'+J_\sigma A_--A_+J_\sigma
$$

is the exact failure of realization to commute with scale evolution. The derivative of every later state in the declared composite with respect to the wall address factors through this defect. Hence \(\mathfrak D_\sigma=0\) makes that terminal state independent of wall placement but does not make a noninvertible \(J_\sigma\) reversible. When \(\mathfrak D_\sigma\neq0\), its evolved action on a supplied state must still survive the finite observable and nuisance kernels; this is a first-order statement and does not exclude higher-order identifiability. Engagement, a post-wall mass gap, and an observable fossil are three different proof obligations. A proposed harmonic sweet spot additionally owes a pole, spectral edge, selection rule, or coherent mode-overlap calculation; a dimensional match supplies none of them.

[[oriented-descent-angle-and-emergent-symmetry|The oriented-descent construction]] answers the next question: what non-arbitrary positive operator can act on the wall residue if asymmetry is primitive? For two \(\varphi\)-preserving descents, their standard-form projections \(e_\pm\) determine

$$
G_{\mathrm{pair}}=2I-e_+-e_-,
\qquad
\Omega_{\mathrm{ord}}=\frac{1}{2i}[e_+,e_-].
$$

The first is reversal even; the second reverses sign. If \(P_0\) projects onto the common retained space and \(c_F=\|(e_+-P_0)(e_--P_0)\|\), then the two-projection theorem gives the sharp bound

$$
G_{\mathrm{pair}}\geq(1-c_F)(1-P_0).
$$

This is the cleanest present statement of the Copernican turn. The symmetry is the automorphism stabilizer of the arrow or ordered pair; the positive form is the even shadow of that prior orientation. Chirality is not manufactured by minimizing the form, and the form need not remember chirality. The theorem is group neutral and type-III compatible, but it is not yet Yang--Mills: the pair must be physically constructed, its intersection must be proved to be exactly the vacuum, and one must prove either a contraction at fixed positive physical separation or a positive logarithmic angle rate through regulator removal.

The canonical finite-regulator test is now exact. [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/past-future-angle-and-the-transfer-gap|For a stationary reversible Hilbert-positive Markov--OS path space]], conditional expectations onto past and future half-spaces separated by a positive slab have common range equal to the fixed transfer space and

$$
c_F(\ell)
=
\left\|e^{-\ell H/(\hbar c)}(1-P_0)\right\|.
$$

The ordered projection product factors as \(pq=V_\ell|pq|\). On its endpoint support, \(|pq|\) is the one-way transfer and \(qpq\) is its positive round trip, so

$$
H
=
-\frac{\hbar c}{\ell}\log|pq|
=
-\frac{\hbar c}{2\ell}\log(qpq)
$$

after the endpoint carrier is identified. This both realizes and corrects the original angle proposal. Touching halves share the entire time-zero carrier and lose the transfer information after their common range is removed. For adjacent cuts at lattice spacing \(a\), a finite physical gap forces \(1-c_F(a)\sim a\Delta_E/(\hbar c)\to0\); the continuum invariant is the logarithmic rate or a contraction at fixed physical thickness. The theorem is still circular as a proof of Yang--Mills when the history law is constructed from the transfer operator whose gap it reads. Its value is that it identifies exactly what an independent gauge-geometric argument must bound.

The first new localization theorem is exact. The same pair form is the sum of
the two conditional variances, so its optimal factorization constant is

$$
C_{\mathrm{var}}^*(\ell)
=
\frac{1}{1-c_F(\ell)},
\qquad
\Delta_E
=
\frac{\hbar c}{\ell}
\log\frac{C_{\mathrm{var}}^*(\ell)}
{C_{\mathrm{var}}^*(\ell)-1}.
$$

This turns the angle into a precise statement about how the two local
presentations fail to retain one whole-history distinction. An entropy
quasi-factorization with constant \(C_{\mathrm{ent}}\) implies
\(c_F\leq1-C_{\mathrm{ent}}^{-1}\) after linearization, but equality must not
be assumed. Maximal-correlation squared is the local Hessian coefficient of
a one-channel relative-entropy contraction; the global information
contraction can be strictly larger.

[[two-slice-innovation-geometry/inq|The second new theorem]] is the
noncircular assembly route. For complete orthogonal endpoint innovations
\(D_i^X,D_j^Y\) of the actual pair law and
\(C_{ij}\geq\|D_i^XPD_j^Y\|\),

$$
\rho(\mathsf J)
\leq
\|C\|_{\ell^2\to\ell^2}
\leq
\sqrt{
\left(\max_i\sum_jC_{ij}\right)
\left(\max_j\sum_iC_{ij}\right)}.
$$

This is sharp from the scalar block data alone. It is stronger than checking
ordinary block marginals because its innovations are centered relative to a
complete filtration and retain their boundary influence. The distinction is
forced by an exact counterexample on \(\{-1,+1\}^n\):

$$
P_\varepsilon
=
\Pi+(1-\varepsilon)|\chi\rangle\langle\chi|,
\qquad
\chi(x)=\prod_i x_i.
$$

The kernel density \(1+(1-\varepsilon)\chi(x)\chi(y)\) is everywhere at
least \(\varepsilon\), every proper output block is independent of the whole
input slice, and the stationary one-slice measure is product, yet
\(\rho(P_\varepsilon)=1-\varepsilon\). Thus strict positivity, perfect
proper-block decorrelation, and an optimal one-slice heat-bath gap can
coexist with a vanishing physical transfer gap. Any valid sampler route
needs a separate comparison such as
\(I-P^*P\geq\eta(I-Q)\).

For the Wilson sandwich \(T=M_aKM_a\), write \(T\psi=\lambda_0\psi\) and
\(b=a\psi\). The exact physical Doob pair law is

$$
\mathrm d\mathsf J_T(U,V)
=
\lambda_0^{-1}b(U)k(U,V)b(V)
\,\mathrm d\mu(U)\mathrm d\mu(V).
$$

The kinetic kernel \(k\) factorizes before gauge restriction; the Perron
dressing \(b\) is the unresolved carrier of nonlocal vacuum dependence. The
sharp new target is therefore a boundary-uniform subunit innovation matrix
for this actual pair law at fixed physical slab thickness, or the calibrated
adjacent-slice condition

$$
\liminf_{a\downarrow0}\inf_L
\frac{\hbar c}
{a_{\tau,a}\Lambda_{\mathrm{YM}}^{(\mathsf s)}}
\,\bigl[-\log\|C_{a,L}\|_2\bigr]
>0.
$$

[[gauge-cycle-innovation-filtration/inq|The gauge-cycle theorem]] now
closes the kinematic phrase “complete physical filtration” at every finite
Wilson regulator. If \(\mathscr F_A\) is generated by a selected edge set,
then \(\mathscr F_A\) is stable under vertex gauge transformations.
Invariance of the actual marginal state and uniqueness of conditional
expectation give

$$
U_gE_A=E_AU_g.
$$

Thus \(E_A\) restricts to an orthogonal conditional expectation on the
gauge-invariant carrier. Order a spanning tree first. Every forest-stage
range is only the constant line, while adjoining the chords
\(c_1,\ldots,c_r\), \(r=b_1(\Gamma)\), gives projections \(F_j\) satisfying

$$
I-P_0=\sum_{j=1}^r(F_j-F_{j-1})
$$

orthogonally. In maximal-tree coordinates the same tower consists of all
joint functions of the first \(j\) fundamental holonomies invariant under
one residual simultaneous conjugation. This must not be replaced by the
tensor product of individual conjugacy-class algebras: mixed invariants such
as \(\operatorname{Tr}(h_1h_2)\) contain relative information.

For product Haar measure and a central product transfer, the chord
innovations are reducing spin-network support blocks. Their matrix is
diagonal and its norm is the exact physical contraction; for the identical
\(SU(2)\) Wilson kernel it reproduces
\(\rho=[I_2(x)/I_1(x)]^{g(\Gamma)}\). For the magnetic sandwich, the actual
vacuum-weighted expectations and Doob transfer generate off-diagonal
entries. With

$$
r_{a,L}=\max_i c_{ii},\qquad
R_{a,L}=\max_i\sum_{j\ne i}c_{ij},\qquad
S_{a,L}=\max_j\sum_{i\ne j}c_{ij},
$$

the concrete remaining estimate is

$$
\rho_{\mathrm{GI}}(P_T)
\leq
r_{a,L}+\sqrt{R_{a,L}S_{a,L}}.
$$

The first term is persistence of each closed relational innovation; the
second is the total interaction-induced influence among different closure
events. Proving their sum below one with the required rate is the analytic
problem. The filtration uses the marginal induced from the **whole** Wilson
state, not an independently written smaller-lattice action. Strict physical
subgraphs also omit open boundary edge modes, so a local proof may require
extended boundary frames followed by Gauss gluing. Neither qualification
reopens the finite-carrier completeness question.

[[gauge-boundary-frame-gluing/inq|The boundary-frame theorem]] now makes
that qualification exact. For \(E=A\sqcup B\) and
\(\partial=V_A\cap V_B\),

$$
\mathcal H_{\mathrm{GI}}
\cong
\left(
\mathcal H_A^{\mathrm{ext}}\widehat\otimes
\mathcal H_B^{\mathrm{ext}}
\right)^{G^\partial}
\cong
\widehat\bigoplus_\lambda
M_{A,\lambda}\widehat\otimes M_{B,\lambda^*}.
$$

Separate regional gauge closure selects only \(\lambda=\mathbf1\); it can
erase a globally closed loop assembled from two dual charged paths. For a
faithful interacting Wilson/Perron density, the exact correlation
half-density \(r^{1/2}\) intertwines its physical carrier with the tensor
product of the two regional marginal carriers, but it is nonfactorizing and
sends the vacuum vector to \(r^{1/2}\).

[[vacuum-aligned-innovation-completion/inq|The vacuum-alignment theorem]]
now closes the finite carrier construction. If \(D_i^A,D_j^B\) are
boundary-equivariant regional innovations, \(Z_\lambda^A,Z_{\lambda^*}^B\)
are the boundary isotypic projections, and \(Q_\partial\) imposes diagonal
Gauss matching, then

$$
E_{ij\lambda}
=
\left.
Q_\partial
\bigl[
(D_i^AZ_\lambda^A)\otimes(D_j^BZ_{\lambda^*}^B)
\bigr]
Q_\partial
\right|_{\mathcal H_{\mathrm{GI}}}
$$

are canonical mutually orthogonal physical projections with strong sum one.
Put \(\omega=r^{1/2}\),
\(\omega_\alpha=E_\alpha\omega\), and
\(p_\alpha=\|\omega_\alpha\|^2\), where
\(\alpha=(i,j,\lambda)\). The exact centered resolution is

$$
\boxed{
I-P_\omega
=
\left(
P_{\overline{\operatorname{span}}\{\omega_\alpha\}}-P_\omega
\right)
+
\sum_\alpha
\left(
E_\alpha-P_{\mathbb C\omega_\alpha}
\right).}
$$

The first summand is the vacuum-balance block and is canonically
\(L^2(J,p)\ominus\mathbb C1\); the others are internally centered blocks.
The split is the same Hilbert geometry as total variance: between-block
distinction plus within-block distinction. It is not a probability law for
ontology. In the correlated two-link \(\mathbb Z_2\) receipt every internal
block has centered rank zero, yet the balance block is rank one and carries
the full nonvacuum transfer eigenvalue. Thus omitting balance can literally
replace the physical centered carrier by zero.

The remaining quantitative problem is correspondingly sharper. Form the
block matrix from every nonzero internal projection and the balance
projection. Its norm bounds the full centered transfer norm, and its
balance-to-balance and balance-to-internal entries are load-bearing. The
continuum target is a uniform subunit norm at fixed physical slab thickness,
or the equivalent positive calibrated logarithmic rate for adjacent slices.
Raw regional charge projectors, or only the charge-pure diagonal entries,
cannot certify that target.

[[vacuum-balance-fisher-geometry/inq|The Fisher-balance theorem]] identifies
the informational content without collapsing its types. Under the canonical
unitary \(U_p:L^2(J,p)\to\mathcal V_\omega\), the real balance carrier is the
Fisher score tangent of the block-weight law. If \(b=U_pf\) is an actual
normalized amplitude velocity, the induced probability velocity has
Fisher norm \(4\|b\|^2\), and the coincidence Hessian of
\(D(p(t)\Vert p)\) is the same \(4\|b\|^2\). The imaginary direction
\(iU_pf\) changes relative phase while leaving every \(p_\alpha\) fixed.
Thus the full complex balance carrier is larger than the classical
probability simplex.

There is also an exact operator answer to what acts on this carrier. On
\(\mathcal B_\omega\oplus\mathcal I_\omega\), write the centered positive
transfer as

$$
T_0=
\begin{pmatrix}
A&X\\X^*&D
\end{pmatrix}.
$$

When \(\|D\|<1\), the short of \(I-T_0\) to balance is

$$
S_{\mathcal B}
=
(I-A)-X(I-D)^{-1}X^*,
$$

the least full one-slab defect over all internally centered extensions of a
fixed balance vector. Cross-channel excursions therefore reduce, rather
than create, visible stiffness. Moreover, unless balance reduces the full
semigroup,

$$
Q_{\mathcal B}T_{2\ell}Q_{\mathcal B}
=
(Q_{\mathcal B}T_\ell Q_{\mathcal B})^2
+
(Q_{\mathcal B}T_\ell Q_{\mathcal I})
(Q_{\mathcal I}T_\ell Q_{\mathcal B}),
$$

so its compression is not a process and its one-step logarithm is not a
generator. A label-space Markov interpretation requires the additional
positive-cone and lumpability properties. Finally,
\(P_\omega+e^{-\gamma\ell}(I-P_\omega)\) has the same vacuum law and Fisher
geometry for every \(\gamma\geq0\). Static ambiguity therefore supplies the
metric on distinctions but cannot choose their physical attenuation rate.

Type III does not invalidate the Hilbert theorem, but it changes its
algebraic type. The safe lift is a pointed normal correspondence
\({}_N\mathcal K_N\) with an atomic channel algebra
\(\mathcal D\subseteq\operatorname{End}_{N-N}(\mathcal K)\). For an expected
inclusion \(N\subseteq M\), the natural home is the higher relative
commutant \(N'\cap M_1\). On the bare identity correspondence of a factor the
endomorphism algebra is only \(Z(M)=\mathbb C\), so nontrivial blocks require
an enlarged boundary or inclusion correspondence. Even then, the rank-one
vacuum corrections are generally not bimodular, observable-algebra
projections, expectations, or CP maps. A sharp Type-III cut has no density
matrix; under a separately proved split isomorphism, \(r^{1/2}\) is replaced
by the correlated state's natural-cone vector. Raw nested AQFT local
expectations remain obstructed by Reeh--Schlieder and Takesaki modular
invariance. The lift is killed if the higher relative commutant collapses to
scalars, becomes diffuse without an operator-kernel replacement, fails to
intertwine OS transfer, or loses the complete fixed-slab norm bound.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/phase-modulus-pointing-and-euclidean-dwell|The phase--modulus theorem]] gives the cleanest integration of the new clues. The spectral projections are available alternatives, \(\mu_\psi(B)=\langle\psi,E_H(B)\psi\rangle\) is their state-dependent weighting, an obtained character plus a stable record is a fact, and \(E_H((0,\Delta))=0\) is a state-independent support exclusion. A nontrivial distinction needs an ambient contrary, but the obtained fact need not have positive entropy. Pointing alone does not imply a gap: a one-dimensional kernel can coexist with continuous spectrum down to zero.

On the vacuum complement, the integrated positive return is the possibly unbounded monotone quadratic form

$$
\mathcal D_{\mathrm E}
:=
\int_0^\infty e^{-2\ell H/(\hbar c)}\,\mathrm d\ell
=
\frac{\hbar c}{2}H^{-1}.
$$

Therefore \(H\geq\Delta_E I\) exactly when \(\mathcal D_{\mathrm E}\leq\hbar c/(2\Delta_E)I\), with \(\|\mathcal D_{\mathrm E}\|=\hbar c/(2\Delta_E)\) for the optimal gap. This is a rigorous meaning of a clock-energy gap as an inverse Euclidean persistence scale. It becomes mass only after Poincare reconstruction and the Casimir equivalence; it is not a pixel of spacetime or a duration between factual events. A compatible complex structure supplies a quarter-turn and turns a self-adjoint generator into a skew-adjoint unitary generator, but complexifying real subspaces leaves their Friedrichs angle unchanged. Likewise a unitary phase decoration leaves \(A^*A\) unchanged. The imaginary unit and the polar factor carry oriented presentation; the positive modulus carries the attenuation magnitude.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/mass-as-a-calibrated-distinction-rate|The calibrated-rate note]] differentiates this statement. With \(R(\ell)=-\log\|e^{-\ell H/(\hbar c)}(1-P_0)\|\), the exact quotient is \(\mathrm dR/\mathrm d\ell=\Delta_E/(\hbar c)\), or \((\mathrm d/\mathrm d\tau)R(c\tau)=\Delta_E/\hbar\). This is the slowest spectral decay exponent and hence a uniform lower attenuation rate; individual directions may decay faster. The numerator is a dimensionless persistence depth; \(\hbar,c\), and Poincare reconstruction are the conversions to energy and mass. Acceleration divided by \(c\) is instead a rapidity rate. Conditional on the classical Schwarzschild formulas and leading semiclassical area law, the algebraic relations are especially clean: \(\omega_M\alpha_H=1/(4t_P^2)\) while \(S_{\mathrm{BH}}/k_B=\pi\omega_M/\alpha_H\). Within that comparison, entropy may be identified as a capacity proportional to a ratio of rates, not a count of factual events. A literal factification claim must additionally construct a positive self-adjoint settling exponent, coverage on its nonsettled complement, an outcome and record structure, independent clock and action solders, and a same-carrier comparison with physical energy.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/hbar-clock-and-the-calibration-firewall|The \(\hbar\)/clock firewall]] prevents this exact retyping from becoming an origin myth. If \(S_s=e^{-sK}\) is primitive, then \(s'=\lambda s\), \(K'=K/\lambda\) leaves the family unchanged; no absolute frequency exists before a parameter normalization. A clock solder first produces \(\Omega_K=(\mathrm ds/\mathrm d\tau_E)K\), OS or another bridge must carry that generator to the physical Hilbert carrier and relate positive Euclidean transfer to unitary Lorentzian phase, an action solder \(\mathfrak a_Q\) then gives the physical \(H=\mathfrak a_Q\Omega_{\mathrm{phys}}\), and only Poincare reconstruction gives mass. Ordinary quantum mechanics sets \(\mathfrak a_Q=\hbar\). With signature \((+,-,-,-)\), strongly commuting inverse-length translation generators and forward-cone joint spectrum define \(\mu=(\Pi^\mu\Pi_\mu)^{1/2}\); \(m=\hbar\mu/c\) is its metrological conversion, not its cause.

The three proposed physical analogues then have disciplined but different roles. The even bosonic spectral trace packages observable terms from a supplied Dirac spectrum and cutoff while remaining blind to \(D\mapsto-D\); this statement is not about the complete fermionic-plus-bosonic action, and spectral organization alone supplies no recurrence. A Q-ball uses reversible phase rotation, fixed global charge, a potential, and a spacetime clock to produce a nontopological soliton. Wheeler's original geon uses classical self-gravity to confine electromagnetic radiation for many periods, generally with leakage or instability; later gravitational-geon work is broader. The Q-ball and geon supply the recurrent-confinement grammar, while spectral action supplies spectral organization. Together they show how rest-like localized stationary or metastable sectors can arise without a fundamental material mass term. They do not supply a pre-clock action scale or a full-carrier gap. In particular, a lowest cavity frequency does not impose a minimum classical energy because its amplitude can approach zero; an action/occupation normalization or nonlinear fixed-charge lower bound is still required.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/cosmic-geon-hypothesis-and-horizon-rate-ledger|The cosmic-geon audit]] makes the whole-cosmos analogy precise without inventing an exterior. For the conformal Killing field \(\xi=\partial_\eta\), choose the future-directed current \(j^\mu_{\mathrm{conf}}=-T^{\mu\nu}\xi_\nu\); with signature \((-+++)\), \(\nabla_\mu j^\mu_{\mathrm{conf}}=-(a'/a)T^\mu{}_{\mu}=(a'/a)(\varepsilon-3p)\). Trace-free radiation therefore has conserved conformal charge \(Q_\xi=aE_\gamma\) on a compact comoving slice or a flux-free comoving cell, rather than energy dissipating into an exterior. Its magnitude depends on the normalization of \(\xi\) and the chosen comoving domain, so conservation alone does not select a yardstick. The flat Einstein apparent-horizon sphere has unit critical compactness and, after importing the canonical horizon temperature and area entropy, \(E_A=\Theta_A^{\mathrm{can}}\iota_A=c^5/(2GH)\). With the stated energy-supply orientation, \(P_A/E_A=\mathrm d\log\iota_A/\mathrm dt=2H(1+q)\), \(\dot E_A=P_A/2\), and in radiation domination the two ledgers combine to \(E_\gamma\iota_A^{1/4}=\mathrm{const}\). This is correlated scaling, not an exchange law. The Hayward--Kodama scale \(\Theta_{\mathrm{HK}}=\Theta_A^{\mathrm{can}}|1-\epsilon_H/2|\) vanishes in exact radiation domination despite nonzero supply, whereas de Sitter has zero supply and nonzero Gibbons--Hawking temperature. Thermality, capacity growth, redshift, and leakage are therefore four distinct structures. The Hubble sphere is not generally a causal horizon, FLRW energy is quasi-local rather than ADM mass, and radiation-only Einstein expansion decelerates.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/cosmological-selection-of-the-yang-mills-yardstick|The cosmological-yardstick audit]] extracts the strongest lawful whole-to-part conversion. Dimensional analysis first gives \(E=\hbar H F(\alpha_H,\epsilon,Ht,N,\ldots)\), with \(\alpha_H=G\hbar H^2/c^5\) and \(\epsilon=-\dot H/H^2\); acceleration, Misner age, nats, and integers can choose \(F\) or a cut but supply no unit. Conditional common count selects \(p=1/3\) and yields

$$
E_*^3=\frac{3}{4\gamma s_*}\frac{\hbar^2c^5H_c}{G},
\qquad
E_*=\left(\frac{6\pi^2}{\gamma s_*}\right)^{1/3}E_{A,c}\iota_c^{-2/3}
=\hbar H_ce^{\Sigma_c}.
$$

This makes the global/local relation exact on the declared ledger: the apparent-horizon whole energy is converted to a local candidate scale by the two-thirds power of its dimensionless capacity. It is not yet an internal Yang--Mills yardstick. Shape data such as normalized acceleration select at most an event on a dilation class. An independently normalized capacity or logarithmic depth must break that dilation orbit. If the algebra selects \(\Sigma_c\), then \(\iota_c=(4\gamma s_*\pi/3)e^{3\Sigma_c}\), \(H_c=[3c^5/(4\gamma s_*G\hbar)]^{1/2}e^{-3\Sigma_c/2}\), and \(E_*=[3/(4\gamma s_*)]^{1/2}E_Pe^{-\Sigma_c/2}\). The strongest current whole-history candidate declares a unit-ledger birth and a transverse wall crossing, then derives \(\mathscr D_c=2\int_{N_b}^{N_c}\epsilon\,\mathrm dN\); neither endpoint nor the history has yet been constructed independently, and its numerical evaluation is CMB-conditioned. A live law predicts \(\mathrm d\log E_*/\mathrm dN=-\epsilon/3\), so constant local masses require a transported dimensional fossil; \(G\to0\) and \(H\to0\) also give no path-independent pure-gauge limit. The Clay-compatible factorization is therefore a gravity-independent proof \(\underline C_{\mathrm{YM}}^{(\mathsf s)}>0\), followed separately by a cosmological selection of \(\Lambda_{\mathrm{YM}}\). Longo supplies the real downstream energy comparison only if the common-count length is a physical localization width; the full spectral inference additionally needs a positive Hermitian loss extension with uniform coercivity on a complex Hamiltonian form core.

The most compact global/local test is

$$
\Xi_m
=
\frac{1}{\iota_A}
\left(\frac{mc^2}{\hbar H}\right)^3
=
\frac{Gcm^3}{\pi\hbar^2H}.
$$

The common-count proposal is exactly \(\Xi_{m_*}=3/(4\pi\gamma s_*)\). Its drift is \(\mathrm d\log\Xi_m/\mathrm dt=3\dot m/m+\epsilon H\). Thus a live common-count law with fixed local masses works only on an exact de Sitter branch. The viable branch is event selection followed by a separately constructed fossil transport; this drift equation makes that distinction falsifiable rather than verbal.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/internal-yardstick-as-a-generalized-rate-edge|The generalized-rate-edge theorem]] now says what an operator-selected yardstick would have to be. Two closed positive forms of the same rate type act on one complex vacuum-reduced physical tangent carrier; at present the cosmic branch contributes the scalar calibration \(\mathfrak b_{\mathrm{cos}}=H_c\|\cdot\|^2\), not a second realized carrier. Their generalized Rayleigh edge \(Q_{\mathrm{rat}}\) gives \(\Delta_E\geq\hbar\eta_{\mathrm{sol}}H_cQ_{\mathrm{rat}}\). The common-count ratio \(Q_{\mathrm{cc}}=\omega_*/H_c=e^{\Sigma_c}\) is not automatically that edge: a sufficient mass-gap comparison is \(\eta_{\mathrm{sol}}Q_{\mathrm{rat}}\geq\underline C_{\mathrm{YM}}^{(\mathsf s)}C_{\mathrm{cos}}^{(\mathsf s)}Q_{\mathrm{cc}}\), with equality reserved for a separate sharpness theorem, and the construction must respect the distinct composition laws of scale characters and transfer attenuation. The dilation trilemma proves that a finite positive edge cannot compensate a full unbroken unitary dilation orbit while remaining gapped, so a boundary or state must point one member. One discrete candidate is an intrinsically normalized dualizable factor endocorrespondence \(X\): if a new natural weld gives \(\log(\iota_n/\iota_b)=\log\operatorname{Ind}_{\mathrm{cat}}(X^{\boxtimes n})=2n\log d(X)\), then the smooth cosmic history obeys the endpoint quantization \(H_n/H_b=d^{-n}\) and \(\int_{N_b}^{N_n}\epsilon\,\mathrm dN=n\log d\). This is a concrete global-discrete/local-continuous normalization target, not a consequence of known index--entropy formulas. An independently calibrated proper age can instead fix the normalization, but it is external unless the whole theory constructs the clock and its unit.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/two-sided-index-capacity-and-the-cosmic-weld|The two-sided index-capacity theorem]] now fixes what is known on the right side of that proposed weld. For a finite-index expectation between infinite-dimensional factors, log index is both the supremal Araki loss and the state-independent sum of expectation and commutant-dual losses. For a standard minimal sector realized over infinite-dimensional factors it is the fusion-additive character \(2\log d(X)\). What remains is not an “index equals entropy” theorem but a choice between two physically different natural transformations. Directly welding additive entropy gives \(\iota_n-\iota_b=2n\log d\) and only \(H_n/H_b=(1+2n\log d/\iota_b)^{-1/2}\). The geometric ladder requires the stronger effective-cell-count action \(\iota_n/\iota_b=d^{2n}\), which additionally interprets normalized horizon area as an effective count. On that branch \(\mathrm d\log\iota_A/\mathrm dt=2\epsilon H\), \(\dot\iota_A=2\epsilon H\iota_A\), and one categorical rung is the integrated condition \(\int\epsilon\,\mathrm dN=\log d\). Exact de Sitter expansion has \(H>0\) but zero ledger growth, so expansion rate and distinction-production rate cannot be identified. Even \(c^3/G\) does not close the typing: dividing this mass-per-time exchange rate by the fractional or additive ledger rate yields respectively \(M_A/\epsilon\) or \(M_A/(\epsilon\iota_A)\). If \(\iota_A\) is independently algebraic, the common-count mass instead has the \(\hbar\)-free presentation \(m_*=(6\pi^2/\gamma s_*)^{1/3}[c^3/(2GH)]\iota_A^{-2/3}\); the exponent is extra whole-to-part geometry. Identifying \(\iota_A\) with Bekenstein--Hawking entropy reintroduces \(\hbar\). These formulas sharpen the target without constructing the wall, its Yang--Mills image, or the dimensional fossil.

The same audit gives the disciplined radical-Copernican bridge. A local state's forgotten background is the fibre of whole states with the same restriction. For a Schrödinger predual channel \(T_*\), the trace-free relative-entropy residue \(\mathfrak R_{T_*}(\rho:\sigma)=D(\rho\Vert\sigma)-D(T_*\rho\Vert T_*\sigma)\) is nonnegative and exactly additive under composition. Type-III structure makes modular comparison available without an atomic trace but supplies no outcome, record, clock, or gap. Moreover, the transverse defect of the expectation that forms an observable carrier vanishes on that carrier. The mass candidate must therefore be a different tangential response operator that charges variations of retained distinctions. Only a uniform lower edge plus independent clock, action, carrier, and Poincare solders could turn its dimensionless descent rate into mass; \(mc^2/(\hbar H)\) is a legitimate dimensionless comparison target, not an identity.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/pointed-facts-and-the-shorted-response|The pointed-fact reversal]] now identifies that operator in the first exact finite model. The visible alternatives not obtained and the globally different antecedents not distinguished are separate axes. For deterministic coarse-graining, KL loss is precisely the expected conditional divergence inside the antecedent fibres. On a positive Hilbert response carrier, minimizing over those hidden directions produces the shorted response, which acts on the retained distinction and records its least whole-compatible cost. [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/shorted-response-filtration-and-the-leak-cocycle|The intrinsic shorting theorem]] removes the invertible-hidden-block assumption: for nested retained carriers \(L\subseteq T\), \(S_L(S_T(A))=S_L(A)\), and the positive stage loss \(S_T(A)-S_L(A)\) telescopes. For a prescribed \(\kappa>0\), \(S_L(A)\geq\kappa P_L\) is equivalent to \(A\geq\kappa P_L\); the Douglas range condition \(L\subseteq\operatorname{Ran}A^{1/2}\) is instead equivalent to the existence of some positive such \(\kappa\). Background elimination still cannot create stiffness, but global gaplessness is no longer an obstruction if all global soft directions are invisible to the retained carrier. The possible mass precursor is a regulator-uniform version of this relative capacity floor on the complete local vacuum complement, followed by independently constructed physical solders—not the quotient, fibre, or lost information itself.

The exceptional-Jordan material now supplies an orientation witness, a symmetry witness, and two exact but inequivalent finite normal-response witnesses. For the selected flag

$$
X\cong\mathfrak h_2(\mathbb C)
\subset
B\cong\mathfrak h_3(\mathbb C)
\subset
\mathfrak h_3(\mathbb O)
$$

the exact theorem is \(\operatorname{Stab}_{F_4}(X)\cap\operatorname{Stab}_{F_4}(B)_0\cong S(U(2)\times U(3))\), not a claim about the full unoriented flag stabilizer. [[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/order-three-orientation-and-the-exceptional-stabilizer|Yokota's order-three automorphism]] supplies the missing asymmetric datum. It fixes \(B\), its centralizer is \(\operatorname{Stab}(B)_0\), and

$$
P_B=\frac{1+w+w^2}{3},
\qquad
I_w=\frac{w-w^2}{\sqrt3},
\qquad
I_w^2=-(1-P_B).
$$

The antiunitary component exchanges \(w\leftrightarrow w^2\), so cyclic averaging literally forgets the sign-bearing complex structure on \(B^\perp\). A trace-two idempotent \(\ell\in\operatorname{Fix}(w)\) recovers \(X=E_1^B(\ell)\), and \(\operatorname{Stab}_{F_4}(\ell,w)=S(U(2)\times U(3))\). The orientation is therefore prior to its stabilizer rather than generated by breaking that stabilizer.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/jordan-idempotency-and-the-stabilizer-gap|The idempotency calculation]] supplies the finite positive operator: for \(C(x)=x\circ x-x\), the squared-defect Hessian at an idempotent is \((2L_e-I)^2=I-P_{1/2}\), zero exactly along the fixed-rank presentation orbit and unit on its normal complement. On the reduced oriented bundle \(F_4\times_{C_{F_4}(w)}\{b\in B:\operatorname{tr}b=2\}\), the canonical idempotency potential has zero locus \(F_4/S(U(2)\times U(3))\); invariantly, its Hessian has the tangent to the forty-dimensional orbit as radical and equals the trace metric on the four-dimensional normal. Separately, the natural continuous constraint on \((\ell,B)\in J\times\operatorname{Gr}_9(J)\) has an exact rank-149 Jacobian on a 189-dimensional tangent, so its kernel is exactly the forty-dimensional orbit.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/exceptional-normal-holonomy-and-the-residual-gauge-form|The carrier comparison]] exposes the important fork. The reduced four-dimensional normal transforms as \((\mathbf3,\mathbf1)_0\oplus(\mathbf1,\mathbf1)_0\) and is blind to color and \(U(1)\). It cannot detect the surviving gauge connection. The 149-dimensional quotient of the full defining-data tangent by the flag orbit is instead a faithful representation of \(H=S(U(2)\times U(3))\). Its normalized representation-trace indices are

$$
I_{U(1)}:I_{SU(2)}:I_{SU(3)}=69:71:24.
$$

The group-valued variables can also be derived rather than inserted. The projection \(F_4\to F_4/H\) is the principal \(H\)-bundle of local presentations of the oriented flag. If \(G_e\in F_4\) compares flags at the ends of an incidence edge and \(g_v\) are local lifts, then \(U_e=g_{t(e)}^{-1}G_eg_{s(e)}\in H\); changing lifts by \(g_v\mapsto g_vk_v\) gives \(U_e\mapsto k_{t(e)}^{-1}U_ek_{s(e)}\). The gauge coordinate is therefore the torsor coordinate of a comparison arrow. The associated bundle \(F_4\times_HN_{\mathrm{def}}\) gives the object on which the holonomy operates.

The faithful character then gives \(Q_N(U)=\sum_p\|\sigma_N(U_p)-I\|_{\mathrm{HS}}^2\). It vanishes exactly on flat holonomy, has only gauge zero modes at a trivial connection when the relevant relative \(H^1\) vanishes, and its positive-character expansion fits the reflection-positive Wilson route. On the structurally distinguished color inclusion, the branching collapses to

$$
\left.(N_{\mathrm{def}})_{\mathbb C}\right|_{SU(3)_{\mathrm c}}
=5\mathbf1\oplus24\mathbf3\oplus24\bar{\mathbf3},
$$

so

$$
\chi_N(U)=5+48\operatorname{Re}\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf3}U,
\qquad
Q_N=288\sum_p\left(1-\frac13\operatorname{Re}\operatorname{tr}_{\mathbf3}U_p\right).
$$

Thus \((\beta/2)Q_N\) is exactly Wilson's fundamental \(SU(3)\) action with \(\beta_W=144\beta\). With product \(SU(3)\) Haar measure this identifies the full finite-regulator Gibbs law, OS form, and transfer system, not only the small-plaquette kinetic symbol. It is an action pullback along the color inclusion, not conditioning the full \(H\) measure on a subgroup and not a dynamical decoupling theorem.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/s6-descent-defect-and-the-chirality-firewall|The integrable-$S^6$ descent audit]] isolates a complementary local/global template without identifying it with color. For the open torus family, the triangle group \(\Delta(3,4,\infty)\) acts through a monodromy representation on \(\Lambda\cong\mathbb Z^4\). The finite-order projectors produce invariant lattice vectors from one seed, while the relative sign is explicit global gluing data. With \((\ell_0,\ell_1,\ell_2)=(0,1,-1)\), the defect

$$
p=12\ell_0-4\ell_1-3\ell_2=-1
$$

makes the formal two-relation cokernel trivial. This is an exact finite model of projection, choice, and residue cancellation. It is not a positive spectral estimate. Multiplying any candidate Hamiltonian by \(\varepsilon>0\) leaves the monodromy and \(p\) unchanged while multiplying its gap by \(\varepsilon\).

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/triangle-character-cusp-coercivity|The triangle-character theorem]] makes the spectral proposal exact on a declared automorphic carrier. For the orientation-preserving presentation

$$
\Gamma=\langle a,b\mid a^3=b^4=1\rangle,
\qquad c=(ab)^{-1},
$$

the winding integers define

$$
\chi_\ell(a)=e^{2\pi i\ell_1/3},
\qquad
\chi_\ell(b)=e^{2\pi i\ell_2/4},
\qquad
\boxed{\chi_\ell(c)=e^{2\pi ip/12}}.
$$

Every nontrivial unitary character of \(\Gamma_{\mathrm{ab}}\cong C_{12}\) has nontrivial cusp holonomy. In a cusp of width \(w\), its equivariant Fourier modes are \(e^{2\pi i(n+\alpha)x/w}\) with \(d_\alpha=\operatorname{dist}(\alpha,\mathbb Z)>0\), and

$$
q_\chi[f;C_R]
\geq
\left(\frac{2\pi d_\alpha R}{w}\right)^2
\|f\|_{L^2(C_R)}^2.
$$

The tail estimate plus Rellich compactness on the truncated core gives compact resolvent for the positive self-adjoint twisted hyperbolic Laplacian. Its kernel is zero for a nontrivial line character, so \(\lambda_0(\Delta_\chi)>0\). Since there are eleven nontrivial characters, their minimum \(\delta_{\mathrm{aut}}>0\). This is a dimensionless global number produced by discrete admissibility for a continuous local \(PSL_2(\mathbb R)\) Casimir.

For the selected \(p=-1\), the cusp phase is primitive. But the automorphic operator sees only \(p\bmod12\): every nonzero residue removes the escape channel, the same-sign value \(p=-7\) is also primitive and coercive, and \(p\leftrightarrow-p\) gives conjugate isospectral sectors. Thus automorphic gapping does not explain the unit topology or chirality. The actual rank-four unipotent monodromy is nonunitary and has cusp-fixed directions; \(\chi_\ell\) is a separate one-dimensional positive shadow. The toric filling fills the complex fiber, not the hyperbolic base metric, and is not the gap mechanism.

A direct identification with color is obstructed in the adjoint carrier. For any \(\varphi:\Gamma\to SU(3)\), the cusp image \(g=\varphi(c)\) lies in a maximal torus, and \(\operatorname{Ad}(g)\) fixes its two-dimensional Cartan algebra. Adjoint-valued sections therefore retain unshifted cusp modes; a central image fixes the entire adjoint representation. The line-character confinement theorem can reach Yang--Mills only through an additional descent line with no combined cusp invariants, or through an operator constructed after gauge reduction and shown to cover every physical nonvacuum direction. This exact no-go reinforces the order of explanation: the observed local stabilizer need not be the global structure that opens its spectral floor.

The commutator cover makes the whole/sector relation visible. It is a once-punctured genus-three surface with deck group \(C_{12}\); its scalar \(L^2\) carrier decomposes into the twelve character sectors. Only the trivial sector retains the zero cusp mode and Eisenstein continuum, while the other eleven are cusp-confined. This is an exact charged-sector coercivity model on one fixed finite-area geometry, not yet a full vacuum-complement gap uniform under volume growth. A same-carrier descent-to-Yang--Mills comparison and continuum scaling theorem remain necessary.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/augmentation-descent-and-the-neutral-sector-firewall|The augmentation representation]] canonically collects those eleven sectors as \(I_{C_{12}}=\mathbf1^\perp\subset\ell^2(C_{12})\), and the generating cusp image leaves it no fixed vector. This removes the arbitrary selection of one character but exposes a stronger no-go: every equivariant operator splits into invariant and augmentation blocks, and averaging to the quotient annihilates the augmentation block. Positive stiffness in forgotten vertical distinctions imposes no stiffness on retained neutral observables.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/global-discreteness-kazhdan-rigidity-and-the-gap|The Kazhdan audit]] identifies the correct quantitative predicate. For a flat connection, a positive edge above the parallel kernel is equivalent to closed range of the covariant derivative. Across every unitary representation of a finitely generated group, the analogous uniform comparison bound is property \((T)\). But \(C_3*C_4\) has finite-index subgroup \(F_6\), so it is not Kazhdan; moreover, a concrete irreducible two-dimensional family has cusp eigenvalues tending to one. The finite \(C_{12}\) phase separation is therefore representation-specific. Global discreteness can change an operator domain, but only a closed-range theorem forbids almost-undifferentiated directions.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/triangle-presentation-descent-on-the-neutral-adjoint-carrier|The neutral adjoint-frame theorem]] turns that same family into the first exact finite algebraic prototype of the missing entropy arrow. Scalar phase cancels on \(V\otimes\bar V\simeq\operatorname{End}(V)\). Averaging conjugation over the two cyclic generators yields trace-preserving expectations \(\mathbb E_A,\mathbb E_B\) whose common range is \(\mathbb CI\). At the tracial state, the sum of their data-processing-loss half-Hessians is \(\frac14\langle X,(2I-\mathbb E_A-\mathbb E_B)X\rangle_{\mathrm{HS}}\), with sharp relative floor \(1-|\cos2\theta|\) on traceless self-adjoint density tangents. The ordered commutator has spectrum \(\{0,\pm\sin4\theta/4\}\), proving that the same relative presentation supports both an even positive modulus and an odd handedness witness without identifying them. The paths are affine and mixed-state, not localized inner-unitary QFT paths. Because one centered matrix carrier has only three real dimensions, a full Yang--Mills comparison requires an infinite frame family with uniform floor \(\kappa_{\mathrm{pres}}>0\). If a natural complex-linear physical analysis map into that family has lower coverage \(b_J\), one genuine localized restriction loss has a Longo-bounded positive Hermitian extension dominating the entire summed form, and the common-count cell is the isotropic localization ball, the exact conditional target is \(\Delta_E\geq b_J\kappa_{\mathrm{pres}}(\gamma s_*)^{-1/3}E_{A,c}\iota_c^{-2/3}\). None of those physical hypotheses has been proved; “neutralized” means scalar phase cancels, not that the carrier has been identified with gauge-invariant glueballs, and the one-fibre floor collapses as \(\theta\) approaches either reducible endpoint.

Gauge twisting confirms the neutral firewall. A center one-form symmetry charges lines, whereas point-local glueball operators remain neutral. On an irreducibly twisted torus the first perturbative zero-flux excitation is an opposite-flux pair with energy \(4\pi/(NL)\), which vanishes at fixed \(N\) as \(L\to\infty\). In center-stabilized Yang--Mills on \(\mathbb R^3\times S^1\), the twist instead exposes the correct two-stage architecture: holonomy gaps the charged \(W\)-modes, while monopole-instantons generate a cosine potential with positive Hessian on the remaining physical neutral dual photons. This is a genuine nonperturbative neutral gap in a deformed small-circle theory, not a theorem for ordinary \(\mathbb R^4\).

The relevant complex structures also remain different. The $(3,4,\infty)$ construction supplies an integrable \(J_{\mathrm{int}}\) with torus-fiber monodromy. The octonionic homogeneous sphere supplies a nonintegrable \(J_{\mathrm{oct}}\) with isotropy \(SU(3)\). No comparison theorem identifies them, and \(T^{1,0}\oplus T^{0,1}\) retains both conjugate halves rather than selecting weak chirality.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/octonionic-slice-groupoid-and-orientation-torsor|The octonionic slice groupoid]] makes the Copernican order precise. Put \(H_{\mathrm c}=\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb O;u)\cong SU(3)\). Although the coarse orbit \(S^6/G_2\) is a point, the transitive action groupoid retains its isotropy:

$$
[S^6/G_2]\simeq\mathbf B H_{\mathrm c},
\qquad
\operatorname{Map}(X,[S^6/G_2])
\simeq
\operatorname{Bun}_{SU(3)}(X).
$$

Thus a stack-valued complex-slice presentation retains every \(SU(3)\)-torsor and every topological sector. This corrects the false no-go obtained from a single global field \(s:X\to S^6\) in a fixed trivial \(G_2\)-background: on a four-dimensional CW base such a map is null-homotopic and its pullback of \(G_2\to S^6\) is trivial, but that narrow pullback is not the general object classified by the quotient stack.

For a finite graph \(\Gamma\), applying this equivalence to the free path groupoid \(\mathcal P_\Gamma\) gives

$$
\operatorname{Fun}
\bigl(\mathcal P_\Gamma,G_2\ltimes S^6\bigr)
\simeq
SU(3)^E\mathbin{/\mkern-6mu/}SU(3)^V.
$$

The link variables are coordinates of comparison arrows after local slice lifts are chosen, and the vertex gauge law is the natural-isomorphism law. Coordinate-free, one assigns an \(SU(3)\)-torsor to each vertex and an equivariant comparison along each edge; choosing vertex frames produces \(SU(3)^E\), and changing them produces \(SU(3)^V\). The path groupoid must remain free. For a boundary traversed as \(e_1^{\varepsilon_1}\cdots e_n^{\varepsilon_n}\), the plaquette transporter is the path-ordered word \(U_p=U_{e_n}^{\varepsilon_n}\cdots U_{e_1}^{\varepsilon_1}\), not an imposed relation. Using the fundamental groupoid of the filled two-complex would force flatness and erase the curvature response. This fixed-graph data does not retain the filled complex's higher-cell or second-Chern topology and cannot reconstruct a continuum bundle with connection. A general continuum connection transports on the thin path groupoid rather than the ordinary homotopy groupoid, as characterized by [[library/parallel-transport-and-functors/inq|Schreiber and Waldorf]].

The same homogeneous presentation supplies more than a bare torsor. For the reductive split \(\mathfrak g_2=\mathfrak{su}(3)\oplus\mathfrak m\), Wang's theorem and

$$
\operatorname{Hom}_{SU(3)}
(\mathfrak m,\mathfrak{su}(3))
=
\operatorname{Hom}_{SU(3)}
(\mathbf3\oplus\bar{\mathbf3},\mathbf8)
=0
$$

give a unique \(G_2\)-invariant connection

$$
A_{\mathrm{can}}
=
\operatorname{pr}_{\mathfrak{su}(3)}(g^{-1}\mathrm dg).
$$

Its curvature \(F_{\mathrm{can}}(X,Y)=-[X,Y]_{\mathfrak{su}(3)}\) spans \(\mathfrak{su}(3)\), so Ambrose--Singer gives full \(SU(3)\) holonomy. This is one distinguished torsionful characteristic connection of the nearly Kähler sphere, not the Levi--Civita connection, a Chern connection for an integrable complex structure, or the arbitrary four-dimensional Yang--Mills connection. The bare stack \(\mathbf B SU(3)\) still classifies torsors rather than connections; arbitrary gauge fields require a differential refinement such as \(\mathbf B_\nabla SU(3)\), and neither presentation provides the Yang--Mills measure.

The particular generator bundle does carry one exact chiral index. For \(E=G_2\times_{SU(3)}\mathbb C^3\to S^6\), the underlying real bundle is \(TS^6\) with its octonionic almost-complex structure. Hence \(c_1(E)=c_2(E)=0\), \(\int c_3(E)=\chi(S^6)=2\), and Atiyah--Singer gives

$$
\operatorname{ind}D_E^+
=\int_{S^6}\widehat A(TS^6)\operatorname{ch}(E)
=\frac12\int_{S^6}c_3(E)
=1,
$$

up to orientation sign. This is a genuine chiral Fredholm index on the octonionic six-sphere. It does not supply the Standard-Model fermion module, anomaly cancellation, a Lorentzian chiral spectrum, or a bosonic Yang--Mills gap. Indeed an index measures a signed zero-mode difference, whereas the mass-gap target excludes all nonvacuum spectrum near zero.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/octonionic-clifford-completion-of-the-color-normal|The octonionic Clifford completion]] derives the response coefficient. The isotropy carrier is

$$
W=T_uS^6\cong(\mathbb C^3)_{\mathbb R},
\qquad
TS^6\cong G_2\times_{SU(3)_{\mathrm c}}W.
$$

Once a connection and a loop supply holonomy \(U_p\), its minimal response is exactly Wilson:

$$
Q_{S^6}(U)
=
\sum_p\|\rho_W(U_p)-I\|_{\mathrm{HS}}^2
=
12Q_W(U).
$$

The operator types must not be collapsed. Clifford multiplication \(c_u(w)=L_w\) acts on an octonionic spinor fibre; \(\rho_W(U_p)-I\) acts on a finite response fibre and its norm defines the plaquette class function above; multiplication by the sum of those class functions and the electric Laplacian act on gauge-invariant wavefunctions in \(L^2(SU(3)^E)\). Only the last two, after their regulator coefficients and interacting state have been supplied, form the Kogut--Susskind Hamiltonian whose vacuum-complement spectrum can have a mass gap.

The exceptional defining-data quotient supplies the further multiplicity. Under the same color subgroup,

$$
T_{\mathrm{fr}}\cong\mathbb R^6\oplus3W,
\qquad
N_{\mathrm{def}}\cong\mathbb R^5\oplus24W,
$$

and hence

$$
\boxed{
N_{\mathrm{def}}\oplus\mathbb R^{43}
\cong
8T_{\mathrm{fr}}
\cong
24\mathbb O
}
\quad
\text{as real }SU(3)_{\mathrm c}\text{-modules}.
$$

The eight is the quotient residue \(10-2\): the defining-data tangent contains ten color-active triality blocks and the presentation orbit consumes two. Numerically \(10-2=\dim\mathfrak h_3(\mathbb C)_0=8\), but no canonical full-\(H\) tensor identification follows from that equality. Differentiating the stable character gives

$$
b_{S^6}=\frac13(-B_{\mathfrak{su}(3)}),
\qquad
b_{T_{\mathrm{fr}}}=-B_{\mathfrak{su}(3)},
\qquad
b_N=8(-B_{\mathfrak{su}(3)}).
$$

The same selected slice also leaves a six-plane \(W=\mathbb C_u^\perp\). Alternativity makes left octonion multiplication satisfy the Clifford relations, yielding

$$
\mathrm{Cl}_{0,6}
\cong
\operatorname{End}_{\mathbb R}(\mathbb O).
$$

Thus color, triality, the metric eight, and a class-six Clifford residue now share one upstream carrier. The type firewall is exact: \([N]_0=8[T_{\mathrm{fr}}]_0\) is degree-zero replication in \(RO(SU(3))\), whereas Bott periodicity is multiplication by an invertible degree-eight class. Graded Morita class alone cannot fix an unnormalized trace coefficient: taking \(m\) direct-sum copies of a fixed representation preserves the Clifford algebra's Morita class while multiplying that trace by \(m\). The 43 added directions are only the singlet deficit, and the Clifford action on the stabilized normal depends on an intertwiner. One irreducible octonion module has no real odd grading; the 24-copy stabilization admits many after a complex structure on its multiplicity space is chosen, but the representation identity canonically selects none. No full-\(H\) bundle lift, graded KO/KKO symbol, finite spectral triple, or physical scale has thereby been constructed.

The orientation cover gives the exact sense in which handedness is a preferred structural origin. With

$$
K_{\pm u}
=
N_{G_2}(H_{\mathrm c}),
\qquad
1\longrightarrow H_{\mathrm c}
\longrightarrow K_{\pm u}
\longrightarrow\mathbb Z_2
\longrightarrow1,
$$

the stack map \(\mathbf B H_{\mathrm c}\to\mathbf B K_{\pm u}\) forgets an oriented reduction. For a \(K_{\pm u}\)-torsor its obstruction is the sign-line class \(w_1(\lambda)\in H^1(X;\mathbb Z_2)\). On \(\mathbb O\otimes\mathbb C_{\mathrm{ext}}\), \(\gamma_u=i_{\mathrm{ext}}L_u\) obeys \(\gamma_{-u}=-\gamma_u\), so it descends before reduction only as an \(\operatorname{End}_{\mathbb C}(\mathcal H)\otimes\lambda\)-valued involution. Its even descendants—the unordered eigenspace pair, Wilson norm, and Killing trace—do descend. This is an exact internal orientation/forgetting theorem, not yet Standard-Model weak chirality: the two color-conjugate halves may be real or particle--antiparticle doubling, and no finite bimodule, independent Weyl spectrum, anomaly calculation, or chiral dynamics has been supplied.

The same trace fixes a non-arbitrary kinematic normalization. On color, \(b_N=8(-B_{\mathfrak{su}(3)})\), so Laplace--Casimir eigenvalues are one eighth of those in the negative-Killing convention. The lowest nonzero single-link eigenvalue is therefore \(1/18\), and the exact girth--Casimir theorem gives \(\lambda_{\Gamma,\mathrm{Haar}}^{\mathrm{GI},N}=g(\Gamma)/18\), equal to \(2/9\) on an ordinary square-loop graph. Consequently the pure electric operator has the exact strong-coupling anchor \(\kappa_NK_{\Gamma,N}\geq\kappa_Ng(\Gamma)(1-P_0)/18\). This is a geometry-derived dimensionless floor on the unweighted gauge-invariant carrier. It is not the interacting-vacuum Poincare constant, and the metric rescaling is compensated by the dimensional kinetic prefactor in any physical energy product.

[[contemporary-puzzles/yang-mills-mass-gap/exceptional-wilson-same-carrier-factorization|The same-carrier theorem]] now removes an unnecessary realization demand. On the holonomy branch, \(N_{\mathrm{def}}\) is a response probe rather than the physical state carrier. Its Hilbert--Schmidt trace supplies the scalar plaquette potential, its differentiated trace supplies the link metric, and both act on

$$
\mathcal H_\Gamma
=
L^2(SU(3)^E,\mu_{\mathrm H}^E)^{SU(3)^V}.
$$

If

$$
H_N(\kappa_N,\lambda_N)
=
\kappa_N\Delta_{\Gamma,b_N}
+\lambda_NM_{Q_N},
$$

then the exact self-adjoint operator identity is

$$
\boxed{
H_N(\kappa_N,\lambda_N)
=
H_W\!\left(\frac{\kappa_N}{8},288\lambda_N\right).}
$$

Thus \(\kappa_N=8\kappa_W\) and \(\lambda_N=\lambda_W/288\) make the operators identical. For their common positive ground state and \(\nu_\Gamma=\psi_{0,\Gamma}^2\mu_{\mathrm H}\),

$$
\lambda_{\mathrm P}^{\mathrm{GI}}(\nu_\Gamma;b_N)
=
\frac18
\lambda_{\mathrm P}^{\mathrm{GI}}(\nu_\Gamma;-B),
$$

so the metric eight cancels exactly against the matched kinetic coefficient. The normalization-free remainder is

$$
\boxed{
\Theta_\Gamma
:=
\frac{\lambda_{\mathrm P}^{\mathrm{GI}}(\nu_\Gamma;b_N)}
{g(\Gamma)/18}
=
\frac{\lambda_{\mathrm P}^{\mathrm{GI}}(\nu_\Gamma;-B)}
{4g(\Gamma)/9},
\qquad
\Delta_\Gamma
=
\kappa_N\frac{g(\Gamma)}{18}\Theta_\Gamma.}
$$

Here \(\Theta_\Gamma=1\) for product Haar. The global density comparison only gives \(\Theta_\Gamma\geq e^{-\operatorname{osc}(-2\log\psi_0)}\), which normally deteriorates with volume. Conditional Poincare plus subcritical Dobrushin influence gives a sharper sufficient lower bound, but its hypotheses remain unproved for the physical vacuum along the asymptotically free trajectory.

The same theorem supplies an exact no-go. Because \(Q_N\) is continuous and vanishes on flat configurations, gauge-invariant normalized wavefunctions can be supported in arbitrarily small sublevel neighborhoods and chosen orthogonal to any fixed vacuum vector. Hence

$$
\inf_{\substack{f\perp\Omega\\\|f\|=1}}
\langle f,M_{Q_N}f\rangle
=0.
$$

The plaquette multiplication operator is therefore not the gap operator. The electric derivative form and the way the interacting ground state weights configuration space are indispensable.

This is a stronger Copernican pattern and the first exact QFT-regulator recovery arrow in the exceptional branch, not a mass-gap proof. The two natural ambient constructions still produce different normal probes, so selecting the 149-dimensional quotient to define the physical character and trace metric remains a theorem obligation. But a single finite normal fibre no longer has to cover the infinite-dimensional OS carrier on the holonomy route: the trace contraction has already induced the standard Wilson operator on the correct configuration carrier. A field-valued analysis map \(J_r\) remains necessary only if the finite flag-normal Hessian itself is proposed as the gap form. For the recovered Wilson route, the smallest missing physical bridge is instead a regulator-uniform lower bound for \(\Theta_r\) in the actual vacuum measure, a controlled identification of Wilson transfer and Kogut--Susskind forms, and survival of that bound through OS and Poincare reconstruction. The eigenvalue-discriminant wall, determinant/rank wall, idempotency normal edge, curvature response, weighted-vacuum Poincare edge, and Poincare mass edge remain distinct structures.

The box-spectrum clue is similarly limited: with its declared kinetic ordering, formal symmetry uses \(a\,\mathrm da\), so its WKB envelope yields proper/dust-clock rather than conformal Born dwell. More generally this result is fixed by the principal symbol and clock choice, not repaired by factor ordering alone. The transfer-resolvent identity above is instead a spectral-calculus theorem for the positive self-adjoint \(H\) actually constructed.

The finite Weyl relation \(UV=\omega VU\) gives an exact flux witness. The phase conjugates under orientation reversal, whereas the squared-commutator form has traceless floor \(4\sin^2(\pi/N)\). It proves that oriented algebra can have an orientation-even coercive shadow. It also proves its own limitation: the floor collapses as \(N\to\infty\), finite-center data vanish for centerless groups, and topological sectors do not exhaust the vacuum complement. The witness proposes an operator grammar, not a preferred \(SU(2)\) or \(SU(3)\).

Twisted spectral geometry fits this dependency order but does not close it. A twisted commutator changes metric covariance and can carry an ordinary index pairing in type-III examples; an involutive twist yields the noninvertible fixed-point expectation \(E_\rho=(1+\rho)/2\). The twist itself remains invertible. Connes--Rovelli modular flow is a reversible automorphism group, and twist-to-Lorentz-signature constructions begin with a supplied spectral triple. Neither is identical to irreversible record order or to the clock Hamiltonian. Likewise an even spectral action is invariant under \(D\mapsto-D\), so it cannot select the primitive orientation whose even response it measures.

The canonical HSMI contraction fails as the missing realization in a particularly instructive way. If \(P=(\log\Delta_{\mathcal N}-\log\Delta_{\mathcal M})/(2\pi)\geq0\) and \(r_\sigma=r_*e^\sigma\), then \(e^{-r_\sigma P}\) is norm differentiable. But the correctly endpoint-aligned map is \(J_\sigma=U(1)e^{-r_\sigma P}\), and affine covariance makes

$$
J_\sigma'+J_\sigma A_{\mathcal M}-A_{\mathcal N}J_\sigma=0.
$$

The nonzero expression \(iPe^{-r_\sigma P}\) appears only when the actual endpoint transporter \(U(1)\) is omitted; its squared spectrum \(p^2e^{-2r_\sigma p}\) still has infimum zero on \([0,\infty)\). This is not a failed mass formula but a successful typing test: canonical modular transport is an equivariant change of presentation. The desired wall must be a newly constructed non-equivariant UCP map or correspondence, not a relabelled isomorphism.

The Type III descent search now has a sharper stopping point. For faithful normal states on \(\mathcal N\subseteq\mathcal M\), restriction loss

$$
\mathcal L_{\sigma,\mathcal N}(\varphi)
=
S_{\mathcal M}(\varphi\Vert\sigma)
-
S_{\mathcal N}(\varphi|_{\mathcal N}\Vert\sigma|_{\mathcal N})
$$

is nonnegative, and its zero set is characterized by retention of the Connes cocycle under Petz's hypotheses. On any explicitly chosen tangent Hilbert space where its Hessian is densely defined and closed, that Hessian represents a positive response \(R\). If a faithful reference state is preserved by an ordinary expectation \(E\), the exact chain rule strengthens this to

$$
q_{\sigma,E}[\xi]
=
\|(1-j_E\operatorname{res})\xi\|_{\mathrm{BKM},\sigma}^2,
\qquad
\inf_{\operatorname{res}\xi=y}q_{\sigma,E}[\xi]=0.
$$

The incoming quotient therefore has sharp constant one, but the form is purely vertical and charges no retained output direction. Away from the adapted state, fixed index still gives no lower Hessian edge: an explicit index-\(4\) matrix-factor family has smallest positive BKM ratio \(1-t/\operatorname{artanh}t\to0\), and tensoring with a Type III factor preserves the obstruction. Moreover, the same preserving expectation admits \(T_t=E+e^{-\gamma t}(I-E)\) for every \(\gamma>0\), with generator \(\gamma(I-E_2)\). The irreducible target is therefore **a physically normalized family of pullbacks with regulator-uniform joint transversality**, followed by the Casimir solder; neither index nor one-channel quotient coercivity supplies it.

Finite-index duality contributes an exact square normalization without closing that target. For an arbitrary chosen finite-index expectation, the conjugate/Q-system intertwiner obeys

$$
\widetilde v^*\widetilde v
=
\operatorname{Ind}(E),
\qquad
V_E:=\widetilde v\operatorname{Ind}(E)^{-1/2},
\qquad
V_E^*V_E=1.
$$

For a factor of chosen index greater than one, \(V_E\) is proper. The Kosaki index generally depends on \(E\). Only for the minimal expectation and standard conjugate solution does \(\operatorname{Ind}(E^0)=d(\iota)^2\); then \(A=\log d\) is intrinsic and composes additively under factor-correspondence fusion. This gives a principled algebraic source for a square character, but a physical exponent requires a nontrivial family of such standard correspondences. It does not give physical stiffness: in a factor the index response is scalar, and the isometry identity preserves all norms.

The gauge audit determines where this finite square cannot live. For a faithful minimal action of a compact effective group on a factor with separable predual,

$$
[\mathcal M:\mathcal M^G]
=
\sum_{\pi\in\widehat G}d_\pi^2,
$$

so the compact global \(SU(N)\) fixed-point inclusion is infinite-index. This theorem does not classify local Gauss-law reduction, and a finite subgroup would insert an arbitrary discretization. The canonical finite remnant is instead the pure-gauge ring additivity--duality inclusion. In \(d=4\), a ring and its causal complement are both ring-like; Wilson and 't Hooft nonlocal classes coexist in the maximal comparison algebra, although a chosen local net keeps only a mutually local dyonic family. If

$$
S_R
:=
S_{\mathcal A_{\max}(R)}
(\omega_R\Vert\omega_R\circ E_{WT}),
\qquad
S_{R'}
:=
S_{\mathcal A_{\max}(R')}
(\omega_{R'}\Vert\omega_{R'}\circ E_{WT}^{R'}),
$$

where \(E_{WT}^{R'}:\mathcal A_{\max}(R')\to\mathcal A_{\mathrm{add}}(R')\) is the complementary-region expectation and both states are restrictions of the same pure global vacuum, then the finite-center comparison gives

$$
\operatorname{Ind}(E_{WT})
=
|Z(G)^*\times Z(G)|
=
|Z(G)|^2,
\qquad
S_R+S_{R'}=2\log|Z(G)|.
$$

This is a genuine four-dimensional electric--magnetic square, but it is a static topological certainty plateau. Centerless simple groups make it trivial while remaining within the mass-gap problem, so it cannot be the universal cause. Its defensible role is to label a branch or organize a family of scale-dependent response operators. A conditional \(D=4\) weld still requires a separate theorem constructing a nontrivial scale family, identifying its standard categorical address with geometric log scale, and proving equality of the normalized response characters—not merely equality at one fixed index.
